| TS_001 | EDI is able to identify the following dates in the EDI 834 file (existing functionality): Effective Date, Assessment Date, Maintenance Date. Enrollment IT can populate any of the three dates above into the transaction effective date in Facets (existing functionality). Given an inbound EDI 834 file, if the member’s eligibility span has not yet begun, the Eligibility Date (DTP*356/DTP*348) populates as the transaction effective date. | Verify AR PASSE new enrollment uses Eligibility Date as transaction effective date before eligibility begins | TC_TS001_001 | None | Positive | Functional | Verify an AR PASSE inbound 834 new enrollment is processed successfully and the transaction effective date stored for the new enrollment is taken from Loop 2000 DTP*356 or DTP*348 when the member eligibility span has not yet begun, even when the assessment date in Loop 2300 HD03 is earlier. | Applicable state for this story is Arkansas and the trading partner is AR PASSE. Prepare one unique new enrollment 834 file for AR PASSE with a new member not currently active in Facets, with NM109 populated as [TEST DATA: member SSN used in the mocked 834 file], Eligibility Date set to 2025-01-31 in Loop 2000 DTP*356 or DTP*348, Assessment Date set to 2025-01-24 in Loop 2300 HD03, and a unique ISA13/GS06/ST02 control set. The file name must follow the AR enrollment naming convention and be available for drop to the CRT inbound location. Processing components in CRT must be available so the file can be picked up, archived, accepted in TM, and loaded to Facets. | 1 | Create an Arkansas AR PASSE inbound 834 enrollment file for a brand new member with INS maintenance for add enrollment, NM1*IL segment containing SSN [TEST DATA: member SSN used in the mocked 834 file], Loop 2000 DTP segment carrying Eligibility Date 20250131, Loop 2300 HD03 carrying Assessment Date 20250124, and a unique file control number [TEST DATA: ISA13/IEA02 control number]. | The 834 file is saved successfully with the required new member data, Eligibility Date 20250131, Assessment Date 20250124, and unique control identifiers. | None |
